ΙΧ Σήμερον ὁ ἀόρατος ὁρᾶται '" ἵνα ἐμφανῆ ἑαυτὸν ποιήσῃ ἡμῖν '΄. Χ Σήμερον ὁ ἀκλινὴς τῷ ἰδίῳ οἰκέτῃ "ὁ ὑποκλίνεται, ἵνα ἡμᾶς
free us from slavery. Today the hand of his creature rests upon the uncreated. Today the Lord is baptized by his servant. Today the sources of the waters have been sanctified by the glorious appearance of Christ in the Jordan. Today the waters of the Jordan have shown themselves (as waters of) healing for men. Today all creation is imbued with the essences of fragrant waters. Today the Jordan has turned back. Today the waves of the Jordan are brought as (waters of) healing for men. Today the sea has fled, seeing the Lord of all things. Today the salty waters of the sea are made sweet by the appearance of their Lord. Today we are delivered from darkness and the world is enlightened by the light of the knowledge of God. Today we have been consoled from the ancient lamentation and, as a new Israel, we have been saved. Today the invisible idol hides itself and human nature is manifested.

Today the superior regions converse with the inferior regions, and the inferior regions are in celebration with the superior regions. Today a spiritual feast of the orthodox has appeared manifest and brilliant. Today we have acquired the kingdom of heaven, for the kingdom of the Lord shall have no end. Today the Savior has come to the Jordan to sanctify the nature of the waters. Today the Forerunner touches the immortal head. Today the earth and the sea have shared the joy of the world and the world has been filled with gladness. For behold, the time of our feast has arrived, and the angels celebrate the feast with men, and the choir of the saints approaches us. For today the grace of the Holy Spirit comes and sanctifies the waters with its breath. Today the illuminated heaven sends dew upon the earth. Today the rain clouds make a rain of justice fall from heaven upon men. Today the sun which

free us from slavery. Today the hand of his creature rests upon the uncreated. Today the Lord is baptized by his servant. Today the sources of the waters have been sanctified by the glorious appearance of Christ in the Jordan. Today the waters of the Jordan have shown themselves (as waters of) healing for men. Today all creation is imbued with the essences of fragrant waters. Today the Jordan has turned back. Today the waves of the Jordan are brought as (waters of) healing for men.

Today the sea has fled, seeing the Lord of all things. Today the salty waters of the sea are made sweet by the appearance of their Lord. Today we are delivered from darkness and the world is enlightened by the light of the knowledge of God. Today we have been consoled from the ancient lamentation and, as a new Israel, we have been saved. Today the invisible idol hides itself and human nature is manifested.

ὙΧΙ ΟΥ̓Εθ, ὙΦΛΟΒΈΡΣ ΞΡ ΑΙΕΒῪΕ ἢ «ἘΘΑΞΡΙΟ, ΠΡΗΛΗ Ἡ ἨΣΙΗΈ ΠΡΗΙΠΕΟΤΈΜΕΜΙ ὁ ΒΑΡ ΤΑΛΓΟ ΤΆρετο Ἀφογσχα τ ὈΞΒΆΣΤΗ ΒΟΛΖ ΘΗ "1 ΛΑΖΣΔΗ Ἢ ἘΛΑΤΟΛΈΤΕ " ἸΞΕΛΈΛΕΝΕΝ,
earth by taking on the figure of a servant, making yourself in the likeness of man. For you did not suffer, Lord, in the tenderness of your mercy, to see the human race tyrannized by the devil, but you came and saved us. We confess the grace, we proclaim the mercy, we do not conceal the benefit. You have freed the generations of our race, you have sanctified the womb of the virgin by your birth. All creation sang to you when you manifested yourself, for you, our God, revealed yourself on earth and lived among men. You sanctified the waters of the Jordan by sending your Holy Spirit from heaven, and you crushed the heads of the dragons that had their lair there. Be present therefore now also, merciful sovereign, by the coming of your Holy Spirit, and sanctify this water; give it the grace of redemption, the blessing of the Jordan, make it a source of immortality, a gift of sanctification, a liberation from sins, a remedy against diseases, an instrument of destruction for demons, inaccessible to adverse powers, filled with angelic strength. You are great, Lord, and your works are wonderful, and no word will suffice to sing of your wonders. For you have, by your will, brought all things from non-existence into existence, by your power you maintain creation, and by your providence you govern the world. It is you who, forming creation from four elements, have woven the crown of the year from four seasons. It is you whom all spiritual powers fear, you whom the
ἘΛΦΑΡΣΙΤΟΦΟΛΟΣ ΚΕΝῊ ἰερΧΑΛΉΟΕῦ, ΟΧΤΕΘΗ μα ΜΕΗΟΤΕΛΈΝΕΙ ΒΟΤΟΎΒΗΜΕΖ, ὁπασ ἹΦΟΡΉΒΙΟ ἈΛΡᾺ, ΤΡ ἜΧΟΕΧ ΦΑΞΑΡΈΙΠΕΝΗΣ, ἨΦΑΧΤΌΝΧ ἘΛΛΕΟΤΆΟ, ΑΕΜΟΜΟΗΣ ΤΟΥΚΗσ. ΤΕΛΒΟΤΕΟ, ΠΡΟΤΗΒΖΉΧΙΜΧ ΟΗΛΆΙΧ ἨΣΠΡΗΘΤΆΠΧΗ Δ, ἈΦ» ἘἈΦΕΡΑΧΟΚαΙα ΚΡ ΈΠΟΟΤΗ τοΠΛΧΒ ΗΔ. ΒΝ ἄστρα, σοὶ ὑπακούει τὸ φῶς, σὲ φρίττουσιν ἄδυσσοι, σοὶ δουλεύουσιν αἱ πηγαί. Σὺ ἐξέτεινας τὸν οὐρανὸν ὡσεὶ δέῤῥιν, σὺ ἐστερέωσας τὴν γῆν ἐπὶ τῶν ὑδάτων, σὺ περιετείχισας τὴν θάλασσαν ψάμμῳ, σὺ πρὸς ἀναπνοὰς τὸν ἀέρα ἐξέχεας. ᾿Αγγελικαὶ δυνάμεις σοι λειτουργοῦσιν, οἱ τῶν
sun sings, you whom the moon glorifies, you whom the stars pray to, you whom the light obeys, you whom the abysses dread, you whose springs are slaves. It is you who have stretched out the heaven like a skin, you who have fixed the earth upon the waters, you who have encircled the sea with sand, you who have spread out the air for breathing. The angelic powers serve you, the choirs of archangels prostrate themselves before you, the many-eyed Cherubim and the six-winged Seraphim, standing in a circle and flying, hide themselves for fear of your inaccessible glory. For you, indescribable, beginningless, and ineffable God, have come upon
ἀρχαγγέλων χοροί σε προσχυνοῦσι, τὰ πολυόμματα Χερουδὶμ καὶ τὰ ἑξαπτέρυγα Σεραφὶμ. κύχλῳ ἑστῶτα καὶ περιϊπτάμενα φόδῳ τῇ ἀπροσίτῳ σου δόξῃ χατακαλύπτεται. Σὺ γάρ, Θεὸς ὧν ἀπερίγραπτος, ἄναρχός τε καὶ ἀνέκφραστος, ἦλθες ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς μορφὴν δούλου λαβών, ἐν ὁμοιώματι ἀνθρώπων γενόμενος. Οὐ γὰρ ἔφερες, Δέσποτα, διὰ σπλάγχνα ἐλέους σου θεάσασθαι " ὑπὸ τοῦ διαόλου τυραννούμενον τὸ γένος τῶν ἀνθρώπων, ἀλλ᾽ ἦλθες καὶ ἔσωσας ἡμᾶς. Ὁμολογοῦμεν τὴν χάριν, κηρύττομεν τὸν ἔλεον, οὐ χρύπτομεν τὴν εὐεργεσίαν. Τὰς τῆς φύσεως ἡμῶν γονὰς ἠλευθέρωσας, παρθενικὴν ἡγίασας μήτραν τῷ τόκῳ σου. Πᾶσα ἡ κτίσις ὕμνησέ σε ἐπιφανέντα, σὺ γὰρ ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς ὥφθης καὶ τοῖς ἀνθρώποις συνανεστράφης, Σὺ καὶ τὰ Ἰορδάνεια ῥεῖθρα ἡγίασας οὐρανόθεν καταπέμψας τὸ ἁγιόν σου Πνεῦμα καὶ τὰς κεφαλὰς τῶν ἐκεῖσε ἐμφωλευόντων συνέτριψας δρακόντων. Αὐτὸς οὖν, φιλάνθρωπε βασιλεῦ, πάρεσο καὶ νῦν διὰ τῆς ἐπιφοιτήσεως τοῦ ἁγίου σου Πνεύματος καὶ ἁγίασον τὸ ὕδωρ τοῦτο᾽ καὶ δὸς αὐτῷ τὴν χάριν τῆς ἀπολυτρώσεως, τὴν εὐλογίαν τοῦ ᾿ἸΙορδάνον, ποίησον αὐτὸ ἀφθαρσίας πηγήν, ἁγιασμοῦ δῶρον, ἁμαρτημάτων λυτήριον, νοσημάτων ἀλεξιτήριον, δαίμοσιν ὀλέθριον, ταῖς ἐναντίαις δυνάμεσιν ἀπρόσιτον, ἀγγελικῆς ἰσχύος πεπληρωμένον ". Βελεη ἐ0Η, ΓΘΟΠΟΑΡΙ, τ ἸΘΆΣΧΗΑ “Ἐς ὙΒΟΈ, Ηὶ ἩΜΕΛΒΉΙΚΕ ΟΛΟΒῸ ΟΥ̓ΛΟΛΈΕΣΤΑ Κα ΧΒΑΛΕΝΕ ἸΝΆΘΟΣ ΤΈΘΗΧΧ. ὑπο ἘῸ ΒΟΛΕΙΙ ΟἿΑ ἨΦΕΣΙΤΗΈ ΒΔ ἘΧΙΤΗΣ ΟΧΤΒΟΡΗ ὁ ΕἸΈ, Ταρεπ ΑΡΧΚΛΒΟΙΚ ΑΡΧΆΜΙΠΗ ὙΈΛΡᾺ τ ΤΆΘΗΜΕ ΠΡΟΙΕΧΙΠΙΛΕΉΜΕΙΚΙ, ΟΥΦΟΉΠΙΗ ΒΕ ΜΗΡΖ. Σῇ γὰρ βουλήσει ἐξ οὐκ ὄντων εἰς τὸ εἶναι παραγαγὼν τὰ σύμπαντα, τῷ σῷ χράτει συνέχεις τὴν κτίσιν καὶ τῇ σῇ προνοίᾳ διοικεῖς τὸν κόσμον. Σὺ ἐκ τεσσάρων στοιχείων τὴν κτίσιν συν-αρμόσας:» ", τέτρασιν καιροῖς τὸν κύχλον τοῦ ἐνιαυτοῦ ἐστεφάνωσας. Σὲ πρέμουσιν αἱ νοεραὶ πᾶσαι δυνάμεις, σὲ ὑμνεῖ ἥλιος, σὲ δοξολογεῖ “ σεχήνη, σοὶ ἐντυγχάνει τὰ
earth by taking on the figure of a servant, making yourself in the likeness of man. For you did not suffer, Lord, in the tenderness of your mercy, to see the human race tyrannized by the devil, but you came and saved us. We confess the grace, we proclaim the mercy, we do not conceal the benefit. You have freed the generations of our race, you have sanctified the womb of the virgin by your birth. All creation sang to you when you manifested yourself, for you, our God, revealed yourself on earth and lived among men. You sanctified the waters of the Jordan by sending your Holy Spirit from heaven, and you crushed the heads of the dragons that had their lair there. Be present therefore now also, merciful sovereign, by the coming of your Holy Spirit, and sanctify this water; give it the grace of redemption, the blessing of the Jordan, make it a source of immortality, a gift of sanctification, a liberation from sins, a remedy against diseases, an instrument of destruction for demons, inaccessible to adverse powers, filled with angelic strength.

Then a paremia is read, then the priest blesses the water three times with his finger. And the priest resumes the prologue in a loud voice: May all those who draw from it and who take it and sprinkle themselves with it

Ὑχι ἐη ἘΦΟΥΣΖ ΛΠ ΑΞΕΜΒΧΙ ΒΑΜΕῊΒ ΒΚ ΠΟΥΟΤΧΙΗΗ, ἴ ΠΟΤΈΙΠΑ ΒΟΛΧΙ Ἡ ΠΟΤΟΠῊ ἨΛΕΟΑΒΉΜΠΙΑ Α, τ πΑΕΑΙΒΊΙΑΙΑ ΔΙΌΛΗ ΟΒΟΆ ἨΛΟΧΗΒΒ. have it for the purification of souls and bodies, for the healing of ailments, for the blessing of dwellings, good for every succor. For you are our God who has renewed by water our nature worn out by sins. You are our God who drowned sin in the water at the time of Noah. You are our God who freed the Hebrew people from the slavery of Pharaoh through Moses. You are our God who broke the rock in the desert, "and the waters flowed and the torrents swelled," and who quenched the thirst of your thirsty people. You are our God who, with water and fire, at the time of Elijah, saved Israel from the deceits of Baal. Now also, Lord, consecrating this water with your Holy Spirit, give to all those who touch it, who are anointed with it, who taste it, consecration and blessing, purification, health. And save, Lord, your faithful servants the emperors, and our most Christian emperor by the grace of God; keep him in peace under your protection, subject to him all his

Master, Lord our God, who honored man by creating him in your image and who formed him of a rational soul and a body of beautiful appearance so that the body might serve the rational soul, who placed the head in the highest place, and who established in it many senses that do not hinder one another, you covered the head with hair so that it would not suffer from the change of winds, and you formed all its members in an appropriate way so that in everything it might give thanks to you, excellent artisan. We pray to you, Lord, God of our salvation, who have blessed this child from the fullness of the bath of your goodness (or perhaps: in your goodness, from the contents of the baptismal font), that your blessing may also descend upon his head, and, as you blessed David by the hand of your prophet Samuel, bless also this child by my sinful hand, by sending upon him your Holy Spirit. In the first fruits of the cutting of his hair, grant him to progress in a completely developed body and (give him) the gray hair of old age,

### Third prayer for the cutting of the hair and the beard

Lord our God, who brought all things from nothing into existence (as) visible marks of your greatness, and who not only adorned man diversely, in your image, with intelligence and speech, but also embellished him with various kinds of hair, adorn with all your graces your servant here present who, for the beauty of his face, cuts his hair or his beard;
ἴ ΟΛΟΒΟΟΧΉΣΙΜΕ ΡΑΞΛΗΎΒΗΟ ΟΥ̓ΡΆΝ, ἨΧ Ἡ ἙΛΛῸΣ ΜΗΟΓΟΡΑ ΞΛΗΥΒἘ ΘΥ̓ΠΟΑΘΕΔΕ ἦν ΤΙ ΡΑῈΛ ΤΈΘΕΤῸ ΟΕΓῸ ΠΟΟΥΦΗΓΆΙΕΙΒΑ ἈΧΙΆΖΙ ΓΛΑΒΧΙ ΟΒΘΕΙΑ ἸΔῊ ἘΡΑΛΑ ΟΚΟΙκ ΚΖ ΛΈΠΟΤΑ ΔΗΛ ΟΕΘΕΤῸ ἘΟΆΜΗ ΑΛΙΧΜΗ ὙΒΟΗΜῊ ΟΥΚΡΑΘΗ, Ἡ ΚΟΥΠΧΗΟὁ ὁζ ΕΥ̓ΧΗ ἐπὶ πώγωνος κουρᾷ ἢ κεφαλῆς". Κύριε ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ὁ ἐξ οὐχ, ὄντων εἰς τὸ εἶναι τὰ πάντα παραγαγών, τῆς σῆς μεγα- λειότητος ἐμφανῆ γνωρίσματα, καὶ τὸν ἄνθρωπον οὐ μόνον τῇ σῇ εἰκόνι τῇ νοερῷ τε χαὶ λογικῇ διαφέροντα ᾿ χαταχοσμήσας, ἀλλὰ καὶ θριξὶν αὐτὸν ποιχίλοις ᾽ χαθωραίσας, αὐτὸς καὶ
him and your servant here present, his godfather, deign that they may finish the rest of their life in spiritual peace. For you are the one who gives to the poor, and we address glory to you, to the Father and to the Son and to the Holy Spirit. Third prayer for the cutting of the hair and the beard. Lord our God, who brought all things from nothing into existence (as) visible marks of your greatness, and who not only adorned man diversely, in your image, with intelligence and speech, but also embellished him with various kinds of hair, adorn with all your graces your servant here present who, for the beauty of his face, cuts his hair or his beard;
τὸν δοῦλόν σου τόνδε εἰς εὐπρέπειαν τῆς αὑτοῦ " προσόψεως ἀποχειρόμενον " τὸν ἑαυτοῦ πώγωνα ΡΟ ΤΛΈΟΜΒ ΤΕΘΉΜΕ ΘΗ, ΠοΑΧΕΛΕΛΕΚΊΒ ΗΜ ὉΧΠΟΆΘΕΗ ἘΞ ἹΠΉΡΟΜ ἈΦΟΥ͂» ΧΟΒΒΉΧΙΜ, πφονθε ἌΗΤΗΣ ΕἸ ΟΣΒΟΗΒΥΆΤΗ. Εὐλογεῖ ὁ ἱερεύς, καὶ ἄρχεται τὸ Τρισάγιον, Παναγία Τριάς, Πάτερ ἡμῶν, Ὅτι σοῦ ἐστιν, καὶ μετὰ τοῦτο λέγει ὁ ἱερεὺς τροπάριον᾽" Σῶσον, Κύριε, τοὺς δούλους καὶ εὐλόγησον τὴν κληρονομίαν σου, οἱ δὲ μέλλοντες συνάπτεσθαι εἰς ἀδελφικὴν ἑνότητα, ἐπιτιθέντες τὰς χεῖρας αὐτῶν ἄνωθεν τοῦ θείου εὐαγγελίου, προευτρε-- πισθέντος τούτου χαὶ τεθέντος ἐν τῷ τετραποδίῳ, κρατοῦσι δὲ καὶ εἰς χεῖρας αὐτῶν κηροὺς ἁπτομένους. Ὁ δὲ ἱερεὺς λέγει ὡς ἄνωθεν εἴρηται τὸ" Σῶσον, Κύριε, τοὺς δούλους σου, καὶ τῆς τυχούσης ἡμέρας τὸ τροπάριον". Δόξα. ᾿Απόστολοι ἅγιοι. Καὶ νῦν. Τῇ πρεσδείᾳ, Κύριε, καὶ αὖθις τὴν μεγάλην συναπτήν. Ἐν εἰρήνῃ τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν. Ὑπὲρ τῆς ἄνωθεν εἰρήνης. Π|Ι. Ὑπὲρ τῆς εἰρήνης. "Ὑπὲρ" τοῦ ἁγίου οἴκου τούτου. Ὑπὲρ τοῦ ἀρχιεπισχόπου. Ὑπὲρ τῶν δούλων τοῦ Θεοῦ ΥἹ Ἔκο λὰ Γςοοπολ»Ὲ. ἘΚῸ ἈΛΤῊ ἵΜμ ἡ ἘΛΟΜΆΦΟΟΤΕ τ ΔΙΘΕ ΞΧΒΡΒ᾽ ἨΕΛΗΠΕΜΈΡΧΗ Γ«οοποῖΡ ΠΟΙ ΞΌΛΗΜΣ ΟΑ»- ὙΠῚ ὧν λαρε" πρεπλοοκαιμπερ Έμ Ἱξοτχηλατο Τ ΛΑ’ ἡ Ἀράπε ΓΤ οΟΠΟΑΣΑ βλιπετο ςπογο ρμουσα, ἘΚῸ ΠΡΗΜΑΟΤΉΤΗ ΟΑ ΗΜΑ ἘΘΓΟΚΈΡΧΗΣΙ ἴ ΟΧΕΛΙΟΤΗ ἘΦΑΤΙΖΟΤΒΟ ΒΕΞΑΒΗΘΤΕΗΟ ΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΙΟ ΠΟΦΙΚΟΛΗΜΖ ὁΑ». ΧΗ Πρβοσπάρταμκ, Ἱποτελμ» ". ΡΟ τῶν προσελθόντων ἀλογάβηναι ὃν ᾿αὐτῷ, χαὶ τῆς ἐν Θεῷ αὐτῶν ἀγαπήσεως τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν. Ὑπὲρ τοῦ δωρηθῆναι αὐτοῖς τῆς ἀποστολικῆς ὁμονοίας τὴν ἐπίγνωσιν τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν. Ὑπὲρ τοῦ παρασχεθῆναι αὐτοῖς πίστιν ἀκαταίσχυντον, ἀγάπην ἀνυπόκριτον τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν. Ὑπὲρ τοῦ καταξιωθῆναι αὐτοὺς ἐγκαυχᾶσθαι ἐν τῷ τιμίῳ σταυρῷ τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν. Χ. Ὑπὲρ τοῦ ῥυσθῆναι αὐτούς τε καὶ ἡμᾶς ἀπὸ πάσης θλίψεως, ὀργῆς καὶ ἀνάγκης. ΧΙ. ᾿Αντιλαθοῦ, σῶσον, ἐλέησον". ΧΙ]. Τῆς παναγίας, ἀχράντουδ. ᾿Ἐχφώνησις"- Ὅτι πρέπει σοι πᾶσα δόξα τοῦ Κυρίου δεηθῶμεν.
For the presanctified gift of the precious body and blood of our Lord Jesus-Christ, that they may commune of it in a state of purity and that they may maintain their brotherhood free from jealousy - let us pray to the Lord. That there be granted to them and to us all that we ask for our salvation - let us pray to the Lord. That they and we may be delivered from all affliction, wrath, peril [and need] - <let us pray to the Lord>. Protect, save, etc. The all-holy, the pure, etc.

First prayer for the ceremony of brotherhood. First prayer for the ceremony of brotherhood.
εἰς ἀδελφοποίησιν. Μμόλ.τπλ-: ἃ ἨΛ ἘΡΑΤΦΟΥΒΟρΕΗΗΕ. ΕΥ̓ΧΗ εἰς ἀδελφοποίησιν. Τ «οὐπολ»ὶ Ἐπορ πε ΝΑΙΙΣ, ἈΛΕΧΙ ΜΑΜΧ ΠΡΟΙΠΕΗΗῈ ἔπε ΚΔ ὁς ΡΣ ΠαΑΟΕΡΗΕΝ, ΞΑΠΟΒΈΛΛΕΖΙ ΛΗ ΔΙΒΗΤῊ ΑΥΤΆ ΑΡΟΥ͂ΓΑ ἴ ΟΥ̓ΧΠΟΥΙΒΑΤΗ ΠΡΈΓΡΕΙΠΕΗΗῈ ΑΡΟΥΓᾺ
Lord our God, who hast granted us what we ask for our salvation, and who hast commanded us to love one another and to forgive one another our faults, Thou, Master, benefactor who lovest
Κύριε ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ὁ πάντα' τὰ πρὸς σωτηρίαν ἡμῖν χαρισάμενος, ὁ καὶ ἐντειλάμενος ἡμῖν ἀγαπᾶν ἀλλήλους καὶ συγχωρεῖν ἀλλήλων τὰ πταίσματα", αὐτός, Δέσποτα φιλάνθρωπε ΡΟ ΑφΟΥΤΟΥ, Τὶ ΒΛΦΑΑΖΙΣ ΚΟ " ἘΛΑΤΟΑΛΒΗΕ τ ὙΦΛΟΒΈΡΚΦΟΡ ΛΕ", ΘΒ Ἐ φλἘὰ τιοῖ ΒΕΞΑΕΛΕΙΙΑ ἡ ἐἘὲ ἈΦΟΥ͂ ΧΟΚΧΉΧΗΜΕ ΒΕ ΞΛΙΘΕΛΕΗΜΕΜΕ ΠΡΗΙΠΕΆΧΠΙΑ ΕΖ ὁςκΑ»- ΤΑΙ ὙΈΟΙΣ ἢῤςΡΆ.ΣΒΙ ΟΞΒΑΣΤΧΙΗΙΒ Η ἘΛΦΑΣΙΟΟΛΟΣΒΕΗΗΕ ΤᾺΟὲ ΧΟΤΑΙΒΑ
mankind, <bless> Thy servants here present who love one another with a spiritual love and have come into Thy holy church to receive Thy consecration and Thy blessing, give them the faith of which one is not ashamed, affection without pretense, and just as Thou hast given Thy holy disciples and apostles Thy peace and Thy love, grant <them> likewise to these, Christ our God, by giving them all that they ask for salvation and eternal life.
ΠΡΗΙΆΤΗ, ἈΛΆΔΗ ἩΜᾺ ΒΈΡΔ ἨΕΠΟΟΥΧΙΑΧΗ Δ, ΑΦΈΒΕΒ ΒΕΛΗΠΕΜ ἜΧΗ, τ Ἔκολιε ΑΑΛᾺ ΦΟῊ ΞΒΑΣΥΧΙΜΧ ΟΥ̓ΕΗΗΚΟΜΧ ὙΒΟΗΙ͂Χ ἵ ἈΠΦΟΟΤΟΣ ΛΟ ΟΒΟΗΜΧ᾿ ΟΒΟῊ ΜΗῤ τ ποκ᾽ ΔΈΒΒΕ, ΜᾺ πολάπλη, Μεῤμοτσε Ἐποσπὲ Ἠλπιζ, Αλὰ ἡπλ ΒΕ Ὰ ΠΡΟΙΠΕΗΗῈ ΞΡ ΠΦΑΡΟΧΗΛΑ ἃ ΤΉΞΗΒ ΒΈΥΒΗΛΙΕ. καὶ ἀγαθέ", καὶ τούτους τοὺς δούλους σου τοὺς πνευματικῇ ἀγάπη ἑαυτοὺς ἀγαπήσαντας καὶ προσελθόντας τῷ ἁγίῳ σου ναῷ ὑπὸ σοῦ εὐλογηθῆναι" χάρισαι χάριν" ἀκαταίσχυντονῖ, ἀγάπην ἀνυπόχριτον, καὶ ὡς ἐδωρήσω τοῖς. ἁγίοις σου μαθηταῖς καὶ ἀποστόλοις τὴν σὴν εἰρήνην καὶ στερεὰν ἀγάπην", καὶ τούτοις παράσχου χαὶ κατάπεμψον" πάντα τὰ πρὸς σωτηρίαν αἰτήματα ᾽ν, Σὺ" γὰρ εἶ ὁ φωτισμὸς ἡμῶν καὶ σωτὴρ τῶν ψυχῶν '".
Aloud: For Thou art the light of truth and eternal life, and to Thee we address glory and praise, Father, Son and Holy Spirit. And then the deacon says: Let us stand and hear the wisdom of the holy Gospel. The priest says (the Gospel) of Saint John: "At that time, Jesus lifted up his eyes to his disciples". End: "And I in them". Look at the 7th Sunday after Easter. And when the priest has finished reading the Gospel, the deacon says the same diaconica. The people: Our Father, until the end. Aloud: Because Thou hast the sovereignty, the power, etc. And immediately he (= the priest) says: Let us commune. The priest, raising the ciborium, says: Communions of the presanctified of the saints. The people: One Holy, one Lord Jesus-Christ. And he gives communion to both of them. And after the communion the priest takes the elder by the hand and the latter in his turn takes his brother by the hand and they are led to the meal while singing in the eighth tone what follows according to David: Lord, <direct me> in <Thy> truth. Lord, Lord, look down from heaven and see, and visit Thy vine which Thy right hand has planted. Verse: Blessed is the man who fears the Lord and who delights in His commandments. Lord, Lord, look down from heaven. Verse: Shepherd of Israel, lend an ear, Thou who leadest <Joseph> like <a sheep>. Verse: How good, how sweet it is.

Prayer for the sowing. Lord God, almighty master of all things, who didst create heaven and earth and all that is therein, who didst command the earth to produce forage grass, sowing seed according to its kind and likeness, and who until now <givest> by <Thy> command food to every creature, who grantest to the sower seed and bread for nourishment, hear our prayer and send Thy blessing upon these seeds here. Make them grow by the power of Thy Holy Spirit, multiply the products of the soil and bless the trees of the fields, so that we, Thy servants,
ΓΕΥΧΗ ἐπὶ σπόρου".
Κύριε ὁ Θεὸς ὁ παντοκράτωρ, ὁ δημιουργὸς τῶν ἀπάντων᾽, ὁ ποιήσας τὸν οὐρανὸν καὶ τὴν γῆν καὶ πάντα τὰ ἐν αὐτοῖς, ὁ ἐν ἀρχῇ" εἰπών" ᾿Εξαγαγέτω ἡ γῆ βοτάνην χόρτων', σπείρων" σπέρμα γατὰ γένος καὶ καθ᾽ ὁμοιότητα", καὶ μέχρι δύ αὐτοῦ τοῦ προστάγματος τούτου διδοὺς τροφὴν πάσῃ σαρκί, ὁ ἐπιχορηγῶν σπέρμὰ τῷ σπείροντι καὶ ἄρτον εἰς βρῶσιν, ἐπάκουσον τῆς δεήσεως ἡμῶν καὶ ἐξαπόστειλον τὴν παρὰ σοῦ εὐλογίαν" ἐπὶ τὰ σπέρματα ταῦτα. Καὶ εὐδόκησον πολυπλασιασθῆναι αὐτὰ" τῇ δυνάμει τοῦ ἁγίον σου Πνεύματος, πλήθυνον "ὁ τῆς γῆς ΡΟ ΘΕΟΓΑΙΒΑΕΜΗ ΡΑΒΗ ΤΈΘΗ ἵ ΚῸΒ ΑΟΒΟΛΧ ἩΜΑΙΒΕ ἸΞΕΧΙΤΆΜΟΥΕΝΧ ὁ πὸ ἘΦΈΚΟΜΙ ἈἘΔΈ ΒΛΑΞἘ.

Ὅτι ἐλεήμων χαὶ φιλάνθρωπος. Μόλ.τΤΕλΞ: μὰ κατα. Prayer when one is going to plant a vineyard. Prayer over any flock. Master, Lord, our almighty God, the true lamb who takes away the sins of the world, <who> does not despise the souls who pray to you; to you, who


[p. 678] {#po.24.p678}


[p. 679] {#po.24.p679}
loves mankind, we run and we pray to you, you who accepted your <faithful> Jacob, who increased his flocks and who delivered him from the hand of Esau and from that of Laban. Deign also now to bless this flock and to multiply it into thousands and tens of thousands; save us and save it from the violence of strangers and from every hour of death, guard it by your holy angels and deign to make it so that he who has acquired it may enjoy it with an eye without envy <and> bring you praise for it. Aloud: By the grace and mercies of <your> only-begotten <Son>.

Ὁ Θεός', ὁ τρέφων ἡμᾶς ἐκ νεότητος ἡμῶν, ὁ διδοὺς τροφὴν πάσῃ σαρχί, ὁ δοτὴρ τῶν ἀγαθῶν καὶ χορηγὸς τῆς ζωῆς ἡμῶν" ποίησον τὴν μετάληψιν ἡμῶν εἰς ἀκατάγνωστον εὐφροσύνην, τὴν περισσείαν εἰς εὐλογίαν, καὶ τοὺ; ὑποδεξαμένους ἡμᾶς διὰ τὸ ὄνομά σου τὸ ἅγιον εὐλογήσας, ἔμπλησον αὐτῶν τὰ ταμεῖα τῶν ἀγαθῶν σου καὶ τὰς ψυχὰς ἐν ἁγιασμῷ διατήρησον, ἵνα δοξάζηται τὸ πανάγιον ὄνομά σου τοῦ Πατρὸς καὶ τοῦ ΥἹοῦ χαὶ τοῦ ἁγίου Πνεύματος.
Prayer at the moment of setting the table. You are blessed, God who nourish us from our youth, who give food to all flesh; fill our hearts with joy and gladness, so that having always everything in sufficiency we may abound in every good work in Jesus Christ our Lord, with whom you have glory and honor and power, with the Holy Spirit, for ever and ever. Amen. Prayer at the moment of setting the table. Amen.

Second prayer for the same object. The priest says aloud: Glory to you, Lord, glory to you, holy one, glory to you, sovereign, because you have given us food for our joy; fill us fully with the Holy Spirit, so that we may be found before you accepted and without shame when you reward each according to his works. Glory to the Father, to the Son and to the Holy Spirit.

Prayer after the midday meal. To you, true God who love mankind, we, your sinful and unworthy servants, satiated with your rich goods, bring you our praise and we pray to you, Master: with earthly goods, make us also participate in your heavenly gifts, through the prayers of the Holy Virgin and of all your saints. For all glory is due to you, Father, Son and Holy Spirit, now, etc. Prayer after the midday meal. For all glory is due to you, Father, Son and Holy Spirit, now, etc.

Prayer for those who go on a journey. God, our God who traveled with your servant Jacob and who was abroad with your servant Joseph, accompany also your servant here present (say the name), Master, and protect him against trial and against all fatigue, and establish him in peace and health, fulfilling all justice according to your commandments, <and> filled with temporal and heavenly goods. Deign to make him return. Aloud: For yours is the empire, etc. Prayer for those who go on a journey. Deign to make him return. Aloud: For yours is the empire, etc.

Ἀτογρχομε ὁςΑΡΥΧΗΜΝ, ΤΕΘΗΜΒ, ΔἈᾺ ἘΔΆΕῪΧ ἨΛ ΒΕΘέΛΗΣ Ἡ ΟΧΑΡΑΒΗΕ ἘΧΚΟγ- ΠΙΛΕΒΙΒΗΗΜΧ Ἡ ΠΗΙΕΙΒΗΗΙΖ ΟΥΧ ΗΕ. Δλο οἐμε ΠροΟλΆΒΗΤα Οἃ πρξοςπαρτοε ἩΜΑ Τπῦε, θὑςτερηλ Η ΟςΧΙΡΗΑΛ Η Τςκαρτλκασο βογχλ». proclaimed that everything is pure for the pure; you, Master who love mankind, purify this water from all defilement, sanctify it by your Holy Spirit, so that it may give joy and health to those who will taste and drink of it. So that in this may be glorified your most holy name, Father, Son and Holy <Spirit>. And after the Amen he pours water of the holy Epiphany into the well, making with it three signs of the cross. Then the priest tastes of it first.

Prayer for the purification of a well and for a new fountain. Almighty Lord, God of the heights, Father of mercies, eternal God, perpetual glory, inviolable light, infinite being, most pure sovereign, who gave to your servant Moses water gushing from the <hard> rock, a source of grace, which you made to spring forth even for Samson <in chains?>; now also, Master, give to your servant here present the inexhaustible water, so that we may have it in abundance and that we may address glory to you, to the Father who has no beginning, to the Son who has no beginning and to the most holy Spirit, now, etc.

Prayer over a defiled object. Lord our God, who said to Peter that the impure and defiled things that were in the linen vessel were all pure, you who have purified us by water, bless also this object defiled by the operation of the devil, make it blessed and sanctified for the use of your servants. For you are the one who blesses and sanctifies all things, and we address glory to you, Father, Son and Holy <Spirit>.

the man whom the malice of the devil has caused to fall into the depths of perdition and error, but who have sought him and <saved and brought him back to you> through the saving incarnation of your only-begotten Son our God; now also, Master who loves mankind, cast your gaze upon your servant <here present>, make him a sheep of the spiritual flock of your <Christ>; you have saved him from captivity among the enemies of God, whose customs he followed in the ignorance of his young age and under the constraint of tyranny, and you have deigned to have him mingle again with the life of your people. Enlighten his reason by your Holy Spirit, so that the spark of holy baptism remaining in his soul may be kindled in him by the breath of your goodness into a spiritual flame, and that the seal of the Holy Spirit may become brighter in his heart and in his thoughts by the sign of the cross of your Christ. May he learn to hope in you and to know the truth, and may he adore you, the only God and the Father, <and> your only-begotten Son,
ΠΟ ἘΙΙΑΕΤΖ ὁ ΒΑ Οβ«ΖΑΆΣΉΗ ΕΓῸ τ ἈΧ ΠΟΙΕΧΙΠΙΛΕΗΒΗΧΧ ΞΗΛΜΕΉΜΕΜΕ ΚΡ ΈΧΞΟΤΑ Χρεμοτοσολ ΤΆΘΕΓΟ᾽ ΟΥ̓ΠΧΕΛΉΜΕΜΙ ἢ ἐπε ἨΔ ΤᾺ ἴ ΦΑΞΟΥ͂ΜΣ ΜΟΤΗΝῈ ΔΛ ΟΥ̓ΒΈΟΤΖ, Κύριε, Κύριε", ὁ τῶν ἁπάντων ποιητὴς καὶ δεσπότης, ἡ πηγὴ τῆς ἀγαθότητος, ὁ Σωτὴρ πάντων τῶν ἀνθρώπων, μάλιστα πιστῶν, ὁ μὴ παριδὼν τὸν ἄνθρωπον ἐχ τῆς τοῦ διαόλου πανουργίας εἰς βυθὸν ἀπωλείας καὶ πλάνης κατενεχθέντα, ἀλλὰ διὰ τῆς σωτηρίου ἐνανθρωπήσεως
our Lord Jesus Christ, in your holy catholic and apostolic Church. Prayer over a sick person. The priest prays: Holy Father, physician of souls and bodies, who sent your only-begotten Son, our Lord and our God Jesus Christ, healing every disease and delivering from death, heal your servant here present - say his name - of the physical ailment that <overwhelms him>, by the grace of your Christ, and make him live so that he may render to you, as is pleasing to you, through good works, the thanksgiving that is due to you. Aloud: For you have the power.

Second prayer for the same purpose. Third prayer for the same purpose. God who have the power to forgive sins, to save souls and to heal bodies by your grace, manifest now <also> your mercy upon your servant here present who suffers from a disease, heal him by your immortal Word, make him pass from sickness to strength, from weakness to consolation. For you are our God who chastises <and then heals, who strikes and> does not kill and who does everything for our good and for our life.

ΠΟοκραΙκχ ὁὰ ΒΖ ΤΡΘΕΈ ΚΑΜΕΈΗΕΜΕ, ΠΟΆΡΧΙΑΧ ἐῊ ἕθα Τρέχει Ἰβταιμημια ὙΡΟΥΧΗΟ ΟΞ ΒΑΣΎΧΙΗΙΝ ἢ 1» ΧΡΈΧΗ ΙΝ, ΠΡΟΠΟΒΈΛΑΙΕ ἹΒΗΜΙΙΣ ΤᾺ ΠΦΑΒΟΒΈΡΧΗΟ ΒΖ ΒΕΗ τ πὸ ΓΟ ΜΈΤΈΕΧχΖ. Χολλταιν λιμχ τ ἈΘΒΟΛΗΤΕΛΙ ἘΔ ϑςταρην τπρες- ΜΟΥ ἩφΕΣΕΦΕΡΟΧΝΟΥΜΟΥ, ΜΟΛΗΙΑΣ ΤᾺ ὁ ΕΟΈΧΧ ἩΛΟΣ ἨΦΑΘΟΤΟΜΉΧΙΧΑ βΑΚΈΧΧ ΤΒΘΗχΧ. ἩχιμῈ πε ΠΑῪΣ ΒΕΠΗΕΜΧ ὁ ἘΡΑΤΡῈ ἨΑΊΠΕΜΕ τ ΘΑΚῈ ὙΒΘΕΜΕ ΟΕ μ΄ Νὲ ΠΡΈΛΑΛΗ ετὸ, Γ«οΟΠΟΑΞῚ, ΠΟΗΟΙΠΙΒΗΒΙΟ ΒΕΞΟΥΜΧΉΣΙΜΧ, ΝῊ ἜΘΡΟΟΤΗΙΕ ΘΕΛΗῚΗ ἨῊ ΓΗΈΒΟΙΕ, ΤΗΝΕ, ποκΆ πλοχ', ΞΑΈΔΑΗ τρέχσι ετοῖ ΘΠ ΔΗ Η τὰ οΑφα πα μαῖα ΒΟΛΈΞΗΗ, ἈᾺ πὲ ΠΟΟΡΑΜΛΕΗΧ ἘΑΛΕΤ ΞΗΛΜΕΗΛΈΣΙΗ Α ἘΖ Η͂ΜΑ ὙΒ0Ὲ τα ΧΒΆΔΑ Α ὃ ΤΕΒ ἘΠ ἘΚ ΤΑΙ
Prayer over a sick ecclesiastic or believer. By covering yourself with the stone of the tomb, you covered all the sins of those who honestly honor the holiness of the Church, of those who, in her and in all places, confess their just faith in you. Our intercessor and our guide toward your Heavenly Father, we pray to you for all of us, your unworthy servants; now we raise our voice louder for our brother, your servant here present: do not abandon him, Lord, to the insults of the senseless, do not condemn (him) in your fury and do not punish us in your anger, forget his sins and heal him of the sickness that holds (him), so that he may not be covered with shame, he who has taken your name as a sign and glories in you; for you are our merciful God, who forgives sins, who casts out sicknesses, infirmities, and sufferings, and who delivers from death. Aloud: For your name is glorified, Father, Son, and Holy <Spirit>.

healing, when they brought to you the deaf man who spoke with difficulty you spat on his tongue and destroyed the infirmity of his tongue. We pray to you, Lord, who have given the model of how to heal men of every sickness: cast your eyes upon us and upon your servant here present who has been brought to you in the house of your Father, and who has a painful ailment in the mouth, and upon me, a sinner, who reproduces the model of healing by your saliva; cleanse his mouth, make the ailment that is in it depart, and raise <-him> from the sicknesses that hold (him) and from his afflictions, so that, healed by you, he may give thanks to the Father, to the Son, and to the Holy <Spirit>. Prayer over a bleeding wound in the side. You who shed blood through the wound in your side, Christ our God, you dried up all the sources of the devil and of sicknesses, and you conquered every hostile power; you, Master, deign now to stop the source of this blood, and grant health to your servant, by the will and the grace of your heavenly Father who glorified you. Aloud: For your most holy name has been glorified, Father, Son, and Holy Spirit. Prayer over a bleeding wound in the side. Aloud: For your most holy name has been glorified, Father, Son, and Holy Spirit.

you raised the multitude of men fallen because of the sins <of> their nature; you forgot all their sins by the fact of being sent by your heavenly Father, by the incarnation of the only-begotten Son of God, Christ our God, on the order of your Father, the God of truth. Deign now to cast your eyes upon your servant here present who has fallen as a result of his sins, who has been struck by sickness, by a blow from which he has not been able to defend himself. We pray to you, Lord, remember your word that forgives sins on earth, forgive him all his sins, his own and those of his parents, remove this wound from his body, fill him with health and clothe him with joy because of your incarnation, your resurrection, your ascension to Heaven, and the place that you have taken at the right hand of the Father. For you are the one who lives with the Father in the heavenly dwellings, you are always with all those who pray to you on earth; be now with us in granting healing to your servant our brother here present, <and> raise him from his bed of suffering. Aloud: For your most holy name is glorified, Father, Son, and Holy Spirit. Prayer over a bedridden sick person and over a child. Prayer over one who is sick with a flux of water. You who entered into the house of a leader of the Pharisees, Christ our God, who gave to those who were watching you a wise prescription, <that it is fitting> to do every good work on all feast days, who took the sick man suffering from dropsy and healed him; we pray to you, Lord, enter now into your holy church and into your servant here present who fulfills your prescription, and take your servant here present who suffers from a flux, heal him of the bodily sickness that holds (him) and pull him from this flux as from the fall <into the well>. For you are the one who raises from the fall the one who prays to you, who glorified your Father who sent you. May he, raised by you, render thanks to you, to the Father, to the Son, etc.

You who received the deaf man brought to you, you put your fingers in his ears while sighing and lifting your eyes to heaven, and saying: "May your ear be opened," and by opening his ear; we pray to you, Christ our God, now cast your eyes from the height of your Heaven upon your servant here present who has been brought before your face (and) who asks for your grace, and upon me, a sinner, your servant, who fulfills your commandments, speak your condescending word and open his ears, grant him to hear your most holy gospel and the words that you have established among men. Do not humiliate, Lord, him who hopes in you before the sons of men, for it is you who give healing to all, and we address glory to you, to the Father, to the Son, etc. Prayer over a deaf person. Do not humiliate, Lord, him who hopes in you before the sons of men, for it is you who give healing to all, and we address glory to you, to the Father, to the Son, etc.

Prayer over one who has become blind. At the moment of praying over him, pass your finger over his eyes. You who welcomed the call of the two blind men and the adoration of those who called to you: "Jesus, Son of David, have pity on us," and who, according to their faith, opened the eyes of both of them and made them walk in your footsteps; we pray to you, Lord Jesus Christ Son of God, receive the prayer and the adoration of your servant here present and of me, a sinner, who executes your commandments and who passes my finger over his eyes, and open his eyes according to his faith, grant him sight, and make him walk in your footsteps and glorify you. Aloud: For your name is glorified, Father, Son and Holy <Spirit>

Prayer against any sickness that increases at all times and without ceasing. Lord Jesus Christ our God, who performed a miracle before your disciples, who blamed the barren fig tree that had not given you the fruit you needed, saying to it: "May you never have fruit," and your word made it wither; we pray to you, Lord, deign now to approach your servant here present who has bowed under your name, and command this sickness that grows in him not to bear fruit and never to grow again; since it does not leave him health, may your word wither its root, and give health to your servant here present and make him serve you, you and not the sickness, make health grow in him as you made faith grow in your disciples. For your name is glorified, Father, Son and Holy <-Spirit>.

Most holy Mother of God, have pity and save this servant of God, now and always and unto the ages, etc.

Ἐκο ΟΧΜΈΡΗΤΑ ὁὰ ἐμὸν ΤΈΛΟ τ Μὰ ΟΥ̓Χ ΝΑΛΗΛΕΤΖ, " ΤΟΓΑΛ ΑΛ ΒΑΞΕΆΛΕΤΈΧ» ΜΟΛΟΗΤΕΔ» ΟὝΣ ΠΟΠΑ» Ἡ. ΠΟΒΑΕΥΧ ΘΑ ΟΕΘΗΧΧ τρέχα.
When someone is sick and wishes to receive the prayer of a priest, let him wait seven days of illness if the illness is light, three days if it is serious, one day if it is very serious, until the hour of dinner or until noon if it is very violent. And if his body finds its calm and he recovers his mind, then let him receive the prayer from the priest and repent of his sins.

Prayers over those who are sick with different illnesses. The priest puts on the stole and the chasuble, he places the sick person before the altar holding candles and bowed, he ascends to the altar and prays to God that He may forget his sins. Holy Father, physician of souls and bodies, etc. Over a monk: You who are good, who love mankind, etc. Over a secular priest: God who have the power, etc. Over one who suffers from the head: You who bowed your head for <the crucifixion>, etc. Over a sick person and a child lying down: You who rendered your most holy Spirit, etc.

By your silence when you were nailed to the cross, Christ our God, you conquered all the words of the demons, every mute spirit and every mute sickness that silences speech, and every spirit of stuttering. Master, deign now to cast your eyes upon your servant here present, to drive from him... (name what it is) and to conquer it, and grant him health by giving him a clear voice. For your most holy name is glorified, Father, Son <and> Holy Spirit.

Prayer over many sick people and over one who is shaken by a fever that makes the teeth chatter. Let the one who is afflicted with fever take a little porridge in the evening, and water on the third day, but very little, let him not take solid foods. And as soon as the fever has left him, let him light three candles at the church, and if it is easy, let him give to the church or to the poor in the name of the Lord. Prayer over many sick people and over one who is shaken by a fever that makes the teeth chatter. Let the one who is afflicted with fever take a little porridge in the evening, and water on the third day, but very little, let him not take solid foods. And as soon as the fever has left him, let him light three candles at the church, and if it is easy, let him give to the church or to the poor in the name of the Lord.

the agitation of the storm, during the tempest, while men were suffering from great cold, while the earth was trembling, while <Sinai> was quaking, you who multiplied the sound of stones and smoke, who touch the mountains and make them smoke, who drive all impiety from your people and place in them all just faith; Master, Lord, send down your word now and drive from this servant of yours this fever which resembles a demon, which seizes at times like a demon, which steals water, which produces a storm-like shaking in his body, which freezes his flesh, which shakes all his limbs, which makes his teeth chatter; to it which afflicts his soul, <forbid> all the moments (of the fevers) which seize furtively, all the hours and all the times, defend all paths of access from it, close all paths of access to it, dry up the source from which it comes, forbid it all hold from which it takes its strength, and establish health in the (sick person). <I> conjure you by the name of the Lord, fever, the last of illnesses, you who are despised, the weakest of all illnesses: do you yourself and all your fevers not fear all the wisdom of the wise of the earth and the vigor of the vigorous, the virility of the virile and the femininity of women, the <docility> of the docile, finally the madness of the mad? You who tremble more than all, flee from this servant of the Lord who has faith in the name of the Lord, save yourself from him and do not keep remembrance of him, do not make mention of him, let there not be from now on in him your name, nor yourself, nor your fevers. For the Lord forbids you all the moments and all the hours, and all the epochs (of the) intermittent (fevers), he forbids you all the paths and all the trails of access, and every door of access is closed to you: he establishes health in the sick person, and he gives glory to <his> name. The Lord adjures you and casts you out, fever, you who secretly steal his (= the sick man's) strength, who hide yourself in darkness like a thief, you who inflict your ignominy upon the healthy man. The Lord casts you out, morning fever, the last of diseases, unreasonable and without strength, who agitate unreasonable men and those of little faith: he over whom, in the morning, the chiefs and the priests of the Jews and the elders of the Jews held counsel; who was bound by the high priests and the elders of the Jews in the morning council, and was delivered to Pontius Pilate. Fear him and come out: remember Judas Iscariot who was afraid, who returned the money and who hanged himself; remember the scribes who did not dare to accept the money of his price; of him who stood before Pilate, and he was slandered by the scribes and the elders; of him who greatly astonished Pilate; remember the (wife) of Pilate who was afraid of Jesus the Just, and be struck with terror; remember Pilate who was afraid and who said: "I am innocent of the blood of this Just man". Tremble, flee, the Lord casts you out, he who was loaded with bonds in the morning council, and he gives health to this servant of his.

Prayer over one who is agitated by fever in the morning. The Lord casts you out, morning fever, the last of diseases, unreasonable and without strength, who agitate unreasonable men and those of little faith: he over whom, in the morning, the chiefs and the priests of the Jews and the elders of the Jews held counsel; who was bound by the high priests and the elders of the Jews in the morning council, and was delivered to Pontius Pilate. Fear him and come out: remember Judas Iscariot who was afraid, who returned the money and who hanged himself; remember the scribes who did not dare to accept the money of his price; of him who stood before Pilate, and he was slandered by the scribes and the elders; of him who greatly astonished Pilate; remember the (wife) of Pilate who was afraid of Jesus the Just, and be struck with terror; remember Pilate who was afraid and who said: "I am innocent of the blood of this Just man". Tremble, flee, the Lord casts you out, he who was loaded with bonds in the morning council, and he gives health to this servant of his.

I adjure you by the almighty God, who inspired the prophets with the divine Spirit and assisted the apostles, and who filled the whole earth with piety: fear, flee, save yourself, depart, every impure and defiled demon, subterranean, of the abyss, <liar of Tartarus>, cunning in perfidy, formless, multiform, visible through impudence, invisible through hypocrisy; wherever you are and from wherever you come, whether you are Beelzebub himself, or invisible (?), or proud, or appearing in the guise of an animal, or in the figure of a serpent or a beast, or showing yourself as a vapor or some smoke, or flaming like a fire, or manifesting yourself in a vision as (behold) a man, or a woman, or as a reptile, or a bird, or speaking at night, or <deaf, or frightening by cutting the road>, or conspiring in garlic, or drunk in wine (taken) in large quantity, or in sickness, or in languor, or in tears

χατάθασις τῆς Τριάδος τὴν ἕνωσιν ἐμαρτύρησεν. Ὁρκίζω σε κατ᾽ ἐχείνου ὁ τοῦ ἄνεμον" ΟΤΒΟΒΑ᾽ ΞΑΠΡἘΙΒΕΙΠΉΗΙΙΙ. or from the tree, or from the wooded grove, or from the bird, or from the <thunder>, or from the hail, or from the <lightning>, or from the foundations of the house, or from the roof of the baths, or from the pool of water, or from the tomb of an idolater, or from places we know and those we do not know, from known and unknown (places), from a doubtful spot: separate yourself and depart, be ashamed before the image fashioned by the hand of God, fear the likeness of the God made flesh, do not hide in this servant of God here, for the rod of iron, the burning furnace, hell, and the gnashing of teeth await you, threatening your disobedience. Fear, save yourself, do not return either alone or with other wicked impure spirits, (go) into the impassable, waterless, and uncultivated land, where man does not live, which God alone inspects, he who chains all those who bewitch his image and who plot against it, who has also cast you, chained, into the darkness of Tartarus, into the great night and (for the great) day, devil, instigator of all evil. Φοβήθητι, φιμώθητι" φύγε, μὴ ὑποστρέψης, μὴ ὑποχρυδῇς μεθ᾽ ἑτέρας "5 πονηρίας πνευμάτων ἀκαθάρτων, ἀλλὰ ἄπελθε εἰς γῆν ἄνυδρον, ἔρημον, ἀγεώργητον, ἣν ἄνθρωπος οὐκ οἰκεῖ, Θεὸς μόνος ἐπισκοπεῖ "7, ὁ δεσμεύων πάντας τοὺς βασκαίνοντας καὶ ἐπιβουλεύοντας τῇ αὑτοῦ εἰκόνι", καὶ σειραῖς ζόφου ταρταρώσας " εἰς μακρὰν νύκτα καὶ ἡμέραν δ σὲ πάντων τῶν καχῶν πεπειραμένον καὶ ἐφευρετὴν' διάδολον.
or from the tree, or from the wooded grove, or from the bird, or from the <thunder>, or from the hail, or from the <lightning>, or from the foundations of the house, or from the roof of the baths, or from the pool of water, or from the tomb of an idolater, or from places we know and those we do not know, from known and unknown (places), from a doubtful spot: separate yourself and depart, be ashamed before the image fashioned by the hand of God, fear the likeness of the God made flesh, do not hide in this servant of God here, for the rod of iron, the burning furnace, hell, and the gnashing of teeth await you, threatening your disobedience. Fear, save yourself, do not return either alone or with other wicked impure spirits, (go) into the impassable, waterless, and uncultivated land, where man does not live, which God alone inspects, he who chains all those who bewitch his image and who plot against it, who has also cast you, chained, into the darkness of Tartarus, into the great night and (for the great) day, devil, instigator of all evil.

ἐπιτιμήσαντος καὶ κλύδωνα θαλάσσης πραὔναντος, τὸ στῖφος δαιμόνων ἐχκδιώξαντος, καὶ χόρας ὀμμάτων ἀπὸ μήτρας λειπούσας πηλῷ τυφλοῖς ' ἀναβλέψαι ἐναρμόσαντος, καὶ τὴν ἀρχαίαν τοῦ γένους ἡμῶν διάπλασιν ἀνακαινίσαντος "5, χαὶ ἀλάλοις τὸ λαλεῖν ἀνορθώ- σαντος ἡ, τοῦ στίγματα λέπρας ἀποσμήξαντος, καὶ νεχροὺς ἐκ τῶν τάφων ἀναστήσαντος, τοῦ μέχρι ταφῆς τοῖς ἀνθρώποις ὁμιλήσαντος " καὶ τὸν ἅδην ἐγέρσει σχυλεύσαντος χαὶ πᾶσαν τὴν ἀνθρωπότητα ἀνάλωτον τῷ θανάτῳ χατασχευάσαντος. Ὁρχίζω " σε χατὰ τοῦ Θεοῦ τοῦ παντοχράτορος, τοῦ ὁ τῇ θεοπνεύστῳ φωνῇ τοὺς ἀνθρώπους ἐμπνεύσαντος ὁ καὶ τοῖς ἀποστόλοις συμπράξαντος, καὶ πᾶσαν τὴν οἰκουμένην εὐσεδείας πληρώσαντος" φοδήθητι, φύγε, δραπέτευσον, ἀναχώρησον, δαιμόνιον "΄ ἀκάθαρτον χαὶ ἐναγές, καταχθόνιον, βύθιον, ἀπατηλόν, ἄμορφον “ἧ, θεατὸν δὲ’ ἀναίδειαν, ἀθέατον διὰ τὴν ὑπόχρισιν' ὅπου ἂν τυγχάνης, ἢ ᾽ ἀπέρχῃ, ἢ αὐτὸς εἶ ὁ Βεελζεδούδ", ἢ κατασείων, ἢ δῥαχοντοειδής, ἢ θηριοπρόσωπος, ἢ ὡς ἀτμίς, ἢ ὡς πετεινόν, ἢ νυχτολάλον, ἢ χωφόν, ἢ ἄλαλον, ἢ ἐχφοδοῦν ἐξ ἐπιδρομῆς, ἢ σπαράσσον, ἣ ἐπιδουλεῦον ἐν ὕπνῳ βαρεῖ "5, ἢ ἐν νόσῳ, ἢ ἐν μαλακίᾳ, ἢ ἐν γέλωτι ῥέμδον, ὉΜΈΧΧ ΠΟΟΥΙΒΑΙΑ, ἩΔῊ ΠΟΥΟΤΟΙΠΕΝΧ, ΔῊ ἈΚΟΤΟῪΜΕΧ"", ΗΔῊ ΠΟΧΟΤΕΗΖ, ΗΔῊ ἨλΟ- Δεομζ, ΔῊ ἼΛΡΧΙ ΑΕΔ, ΗΛῊ ΝᾺ ΠΟΧΟΤΕ ΒΕΗΟΤΟΝΖ, ΗΔῊ ἘΞΛΟΞΕΡΕΒ, ΗΔῊ ἹΛΡΟΜΚ ΘΕΡΈΤΑΤΟΛΕ, ἩΔῊ ΒΧ ΠΗΒΗΧΟΤΒΈ ΚΡΧΒῊ ΠΡΟΛΗΒΑΙΆ, ἩΔῊ ΜΚΑΤΕΚΕΙΙΣ ΤΑΟΡΕη, ἘΞΟΤΟΥΆΓΗΣ, ΧΈΧΗΜΕΖ, ἨΕΟΥΟΤΟΟΕΉΧ, ὁζ ΛΟΥΉΘΙΕ ΠΡΈΜΈΜΕΙΔ οὰ Ἢ Χ ΒΡΈΜΕΜΕΜΙ, ἘΤΈΡΟΜ. Second prayer for the same purpose. We give you thanks, Lord our God, for you alone have immortal life, incomparable glory, infinite mercy, unspeakable love for mankind, and the empire that is not yielded to another, and there is no partiality with you, for you have established for all men the common (rule), the term of their life completed. That is why we pray to you, Lord, to grant rest in the bosom of Abraham, of Isaac, of Jacob to this your servant, our brother who served with us, who has fallen asleep in the hope of the resurrection of life, and as you had made him on earth your servant in your church, ... welcome (him) before the angels without condemning (him to the deprivation) of your glory. You have already (?) glorified his life on earth, consent to make his exit from life his entry among your saints, add his spirit to the count of all your saints who have pleased (you) since the ages.
ὁλλα δῦ μεθολΑΕΝΉΛ ΠΡΗΗΜΗ. Εὐχαριστοῦμέν σοι, Δέσποτα" Κύριε ὁ Θεὸς ἡμῶν, ὅτι σοῦ" μόνου τὸ ζῆν ἀθάνατον, καὶ ἡ δόξα ἀκατάλειπτος, τὸ ἔλεος ἀμέτρητον, καὶ ἡ φιλανθρωπία ἄφατος, καὶ ἡ βασιλεία ἀδιάδοχος, καὶ οὐχ ἔστιν προσωποληψία παρὰ σοί", πᾶσι γὰρ ἀνθρώποις τὸ χοινὸν τὸν ὅρον τοῦ βίου πληρωθήτω". Διὸ ἱκετεύομέν σοι, Κύριε, τὸν δοῦλόν σου τόνδε" χαὶ συλλει- πουργὸν" γενόμενον, τὸν ἐπ’ ἐλπίδι ἀναστάσεως ζωῆς αἰωνίου" κοιμηθέντα, ἐν κόλποις ᾿Αὐραὰμ καὶ ᾿Ισαὰκ χαὶ ᾿Ιαχὼδ ἀνάπαυσον, καὶ ὥσπερ ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς ἐν τῇ ἐχκλησίᾳ σου λειτουργὸν αὐτὸν κατέστησας, οὕτως καὶ ἐν τῷ ἐπουρανίῳ σου θυσιαστηρίῳ. ἀνάδειξον', Κύριε" ἐπὶ ἀνθρώπων πνευματικῇ ἀξίᾳ καταχοσμήσας, ἐπὶ ἀγγέλων τῆς δόξης σου ἀκατάκριτον πρόσδεξαι '΄. ᾿Αυτὸς ἐπὶ τῆς γῆς τὴν ζωὴν αὐτοῦ ἐδόξασας", αὐτὸς καὶ τὴν ἔξοδον αὐτοῦ" ἐν τῇ εἰσόδῳ τῶν ἁγίων σου δικαίωσον, τὸ πνεῦμα αὐτοῦ μετὰ πάντων τῶν δικαίων "" τῶν ἀπ᾽ αἰῶνός σοι εὐαρεστησάντων συναρίθμησον. Go away into the desert mountains, into the trees that do not bear fruit, and give glory to God, with the Father and the Holy Spirit, now and always and unto the <ages of ages. Amen>.

Εἰ δὲ παραχούσητε '", οὐκ ΤᾺ ΒΡΧΤΟΤΡΑΑΧ. μὲ Ἠὲ Ποολογίπλετε νης ἘΈΛΧΗΛΛΓΟ, ἩΛΦΟΚἘΥΆΡΌΚΖΙΑ "ἢ ΠΕΤΗΝΑ ἩΞΈΛΑΤΧ ΒλΟζ. Αὐτηλῖτε ἘΔ πογΟΥΧΙ ΤΟΡΖΙ, ΒΒ ΑΥ̓ΈΒΑ ΜΕΠΛΟΆΧΗΛΑ, Η ΛΑΛΗΤΕ ΟΛΆΒΑ ἘΦΌΡΟΥ ὁχ ὑςτΈΡηΣ. Ἐξέλθετε οὖν τάχιον, ὅπως δῶμεν'" δόξαν τῷ Πατρὶ καὶ τῷ Υἱῷ καὶ τῷ ἁγίῳ Πνεύματι, νῦν καὶ ἀεὶ χαὶ εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας τῶν αἰώνων, ᾿Αμήν. “ΕΥ̓͂ΧΑΙ τῆς γονυκλισίας τῆς ἁγίας πεντηκοστῆς". PRAYER of genuflection for the Holy Pentecost at vespers.

ΠΟ ΠροΚΗΜΕΜῈ Ολογτκλ» με ετ Χ»" ΠΟΚΛΑΒΜΈΙΕΙΒΕ ΚΟΛΈΝΑ ΒΖ ΜΗΡῈ ΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΟ ἡ ΠΟΜΟΛΉΜΧ ὁΑ. 3Ὰ ϑοβκαΡΊμεηηε ΓΟ ΛΟ ἈΦΟΥΣΙΙΣ πρΈλς Γ αοοποράσμι, ΓΟΟΟΠΟΛΡΙῸ ΠΟΜΟΛΉΜΖ οΑ. Οὐ οἸΧΡΑΒΕΗΒῊ ΛῊΖ ἘθξῸ ΒΟΛΈΒΛΗΜΈ ΒΒ ΒἘΦΕ ἘΛΑΤΟΛΈΤΗΙΝ ὁπ ΒΑΡ ΤΆΛΤΟ ἐπον Ατογσχὰ Γαοοπολρν πομολή τα Δ». Οὐ ΟΕΡΕΤΕΗΒΗ ΝΑΜΖ ἘΛΑΥΤΟΛΈΤΗ ἵ ΜΗΛΟΟΤῊ ΠρΈΟΞΚΑΣΤΑΛ-τὸΣ ἐμὸν Ἀςφογσχα ΓΙ «οοποΆΡν “ΠΟΜΟΛΗΜΖ ΟΑΡ.
PRAYER of genuflection for the Holy Pentecost at vespers. After the prokeimenon, the deacon says: Kneeling in peace - let us pray to the Lord. For the sanctification of all our souls before the Lord - let us pray to the Lord. That we may become worthy of the imperishable kingdom by the grace of the Holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may be the dwelling and the abode of his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may maintain ourselves by the grace of his Holy Spirit without trouble in the faith - let us pray to the Lord. That we may find the grace and mercy of his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That the present worship (rendered by) our reason may be an acceptable sacrifice - let us pray to the Lord. That we may be zealous for good works and that we may gain the kingdom of Heaven - let us pray to the Lord.
Οὐ ΚΛΑΓΟΒΧΤΘΑΒΗῈ ΤΥ ΒῈ ἘΣΙΤῊ ΟΛΟΒΟΟΖΗΟΥΜΟΥ ΟΕΜΟῪ ΟΛΟΥΚΕΗΒΙΟ ἨλΙπεμογ ἢ -τ Γ«οΟΠΟΑΡῸ ΠΟΜΟΦΛΗΜΧ ΟΑΡ. ὐ ἘΧΙΤῊ ΑΙ ΕΒΒΗΜΤΕΛΟΜΖ ΑΟΕΡΧΙΜΣ ἈΈΛΔΕΘΕΙΑΖ τ ΠΟΛΟΥΎΗΤῊ ἨφερπςεΣσ ὈΚΕΡΗΟ ἡΦἘΟΑΣ ΞΆΣΟΤΒΟ ΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΙΟ ΠΟΜΟΛΉΚΗΧ 0Ὰ». Οὐ ογολχηπλημη ΓΘΟΠΟΑΣ ΤΑΛΟΣ ΜΟΛΗΤΕΣΙ Ἠλπιειδ ἢ -ς Γ «οοποῖιν πομο- ΔΗΜᾺ Α. Ὁ μ 3ελπλεηβη ΠΡΗΜΑΟΤΒΗΜΆΟΜΣ ΛΜ ἘΖΙΤῊ πρξοςκαςτλΑ ΓΟ» ἘΜΟῪ Ἀτογρχὰ ΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΙΝ “ΠΟΜΟΛΉΜΧ 0». ὐ πεϑατηε βάμζ, τ οοταβλεημεῦ ΤΡΈΧΟΙΙΧ, τ Ποϑηλδηὲ ΤΕθοὰ ἘΛΑΤΟΑΈΤΗ ΓαροποΑσιν «ΠΟΜΟΛΗΜΧ 0Ά». ΠΟ ΠφΟΚΗΜΕΝῈ ΟΛΟΥΓ«Ὰ» ΕἼ 5" ΠΟΚΛΑΒΈΙΚΙΒΕ ΒΟΛΈΝΑ ΕΖ ΜΗΡῈ ΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΙΟ ἡ ΠΟΚΟΛΉΜΧ οΑ. 3Ὰ φοβκΑΡΊ μη με ἐορεχκ ἩΛῸΣ ἈφοΎΣ τ, ΕΠ Γ«οοποράςμε Γ οΟΠΟΛΡῸ ΠΟΜΟΛΗΜΖ ΟΑ.
That the Lord may hear the voice of our prayer - let us pray to the Lord. For our salvation <and> that we may have a share in his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may receive the absolution of sins and the knowledge of your grace - let us pray to the Lord. That we may become such as not to be <reprobate (?)> at the moment of the terrible (and) just descent of his Holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may overcome the fraud of idols by the descent of his Holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That it may come to pass that we are strangers to sin and belonging to immortality - let us pray to the Lord. That the angel of light may descend from heaven enlightening our minds and our hearts - let us pray to the Lord. That the rich compassion and mercy of God may be given to us all as a reward - let us pray to the Lord. After the prokeimenon, the deacon says: Kneeling in peace - let us pray to the Lord. For the sanctification of all our souls before the Lord - let us pray to the Lord. That we may become worthy of the imperishable kingdom by the grace of the Holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may be the dwelling and the abode of his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may maintain ourselves by the grace of his Holy Spirit without trouble in the faith - let us pray to the Lord. That we may find the grace and mercy of his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That the present worship (rendered by) our reason may be an acceptable sacrifice - let us pray to the Lord. That we may be zealous for good works and that we may gain the kingdom of Heaven - let us pray to the Lord.

That the Lord may hear the voice of our prayer - let us pray to the Lord. For our salvation <and> that we may have a share in his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. That we may receive the absolution of sins and the knowledge of your grace - let us pray to the Lord. That it may come to pass that we are strangers to sin and belonging to immortality - let us pray to the Lord. That the angel of light may descend from heaven enlightening our minds and our hearts - let us pray to the Lord. That the rich compassion and mercy of God may be given to us all as a reward - let us pray to the Lord.

For the deliverance from every unjust <bond> by his all-holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord. For our salvation, we who have knelt, in heart and in body, on the day of Pentecost - let us pray to the Lord. That we may be made worthy, by the prayer of the holy apostles and disciples of the Lord, of the kingdom of Heaven - let us pray to the Lord. That we may obey the Holy Spirit and know him - let us pray to the Lord. That we may all be filled with gladness <while> adoring the Holy Name - let us pray to the Lord. That we may always adore with soul and body and sing the consubstantial Holy Trinity - let us pray to the Lord. That the people may persevere and await the grace of the Holy Spirit - let us pray to the Lord.
Οὐ που ΜΈ τ ΤἈΛΕΟΧΗΈΗ “ἢ ΠΡΉΘΗ ΠΟΚΛΑΝΈΤΗ ΟΑ τ ΠΈΤΗ ὁ“ ΒΑΡΎΧΙΑ Ὑφομηα ἘΛΗΗ ΕΟΤΕΟΥΕΒΗΧΙΑ ΤΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΙΟ «ΠΟΜΟΛΉΜΧ 0Α». Οὐ οςαρπελορΉ μη ἨΛΗΧ. ΠΡΈΚΛΟΝΗΒΧΙΠΗΗΙΧ ΚΟΛΈΜΕ ορ ΧΛΕΣΡΎΒΗΈΗ τ τἘς ΔΕΟΧΗΈΗ ΔῊ ΠΑΤΕΛΕΘΑΥΧΗΖΙ ΓΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣ «ΠΟΜΟΛΉΝΖ Α»- Οὐ οἈΠΟΛΟΕΛΕΝΕΗ ΑΗ, ΜΟΛΗΤΈΘΙΝ ΟΦΒΑΣΤΑΙΧΧ ἈΠῸΘΟΤΟΣΑΧ τ ΟΥ̓ΝΗΚΑ ΓΤ οΟΠΟΛΆΣΗΝ, ἡ“ ἘΟΑΣ ΦΕΡΟΤΒΟΥ ἩςεΡΕΞεσοχεβογομον ΟΤΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΙΟ πομο- πλημχ 0Α»- Οὐ ποολουιπληΒῃ ἨΛΗΧ τ ΠΟΞΗΛΉΒΗ ὁςβαρταλτο Ἀφογσχλ Γ ΟΟΠΟΑΣΝ πο- “ΜΟΛΗΜΖ Α»- τ σΕρΛΑΘΒΑΗΒῊ ἨΛΗΧ ΓΤ Τκβαρτογμον μεν Γ «οΟΠΟΑΡ ΠΟΟΛΗΦΗΖ Α».
Οὐ που ΒΗ τ ΤἘΛΕΟΧΗΈΗ “ἢ ΠΡΉΘΗ ΠΟΚΛΑΝΈΤΗ ΟΑ τ ΠΈΤΗ ὁ“ ΒΑΡΎΧΙΑ Ὑφομηα ἘΛΗΗ ΕΟΤΕΟΥΒΒΗΧΙΑ ΤΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΟ «ΠΟΜΟΛΗΜΧ 0Ὰ». Ὁ ἩΞΕΛΒΉΤΗ ἃ ΑΗ ΟΥ̓Χ ΕΟ ΈΚοΙΑ ΟΑΉΖΕΗ, ΤΗΈΒΑ, ΒΈΛΖΙ ἡ ἡδπλα «ο- ὉΠΟΑΡΟ «ΠΟΜΟΛΗΜΧ 0Ὰ»-
For those who have bowed their knees and their hearts before the Lord - let us pray to the Lord. That the Lord God may be pleased with us and with those who have knelt - let us pray to the Lord. For him who has given us the strength to please, before (our) end, before the Lord, and that he may send his rich mercy(ies) - let us pray to the Lord. For him who receives (?) our prayers like an incense (burned) before him - let us pray to the Lord. That we may be delivered from all affliction, anger, peril, and violence - let us pray to the Lord. And for all those who are in need, Lord, of your assistance and your protection - let us pray to the Lord.

Most pure, immaculate, beginningless, invisible, incomprehensible, unsearchable, immutable, merciful Lord, whom no one has seen, you who alone possess immortality, who dwell in unapproachable light, who created the heaven, the earth, the sea, and all creatures that are in them, who grant to all what they ask before they ask it, we pray and beseech you, Master who loves mankind, Father of our Lord God and Savior Jesus Christ who descended from heaven for the sake of us men and for our salvation, who was incarnate of the Holy Spirit (being born) of Mary, the Mother of God, glorious and virginal; you who taught us, your humble, guilty, and unworthy servants, by your venerable passion, to

Δέσποτα φιλάνθρωπε, τὸν Πατέρα τοῦ Κυρίου καὶ" Θεοῦ καὶ Σωτῆρος ἡμῶν Ἰησοῦ Χριστοῦ τοῦ δι’ ἡμᾶς τοὺς ἀνθρώπους χαὶ διὰ τὴν ἡμετέραν σωτηρίαν χατελθόντος ἐκ τῶν οὐρανῶν, καὶ σαρχωθέντος ἐκ Πνεύματος ἁγίου καὶ Μαρίας τῆς ἀειπαρθένου καὶ ἐνδόξου Θεοτόκου", ὃς πρότερον μὲν λόγοις διδάσκων, ὕστερον δὲ καὶ τοῖς ἔργοις ὑποδεικνὺς ἡνίκα τὸ σωτήριον ὑφίστατο πάθος, παρέσχεν ἡμῖν ὑπογραμμὸν τοῖς ταπεινοῖς καὶ ἁμαρτωλοῖς καὶ ἀναξίοις δούλοις σου δεήσεις προσφέρειν ἐν αὐχένος καὶ γονάτων χλίσεσιν ὑπὲρ τῶν ἰδίων ἁμαρτημάτων καὶ τῶν τοῦ λαοῦ ἀγνοημάτων. cry out: Do not remember the sins of <our> youth, cleanse us from hidden things, see our humility and forgive (-us) all our sins, do not cast us away in the time of old age when <our> strength fails, do not abandon us before we return to the earth, make us worthy to return to you, and think of us with (your) blessing and (your) grace; do not take into account, in your mercy, our impiety, look down from your holiness, O Lord, upon your people who are here gathered and who await from you abundant mercy, visit us by your goodness, deliver us from the tyranny of the devil, surround our life with the protection of a faithful apostle (?) and entrust (-him) with your people, and gather (-them) all into your kingdom, grant pardon to your people, forgive (-us) our sins and cleanse us all by the action of your Holy Spirit, destroy the artifices of the devil directed against us. Most pure, immaculate, beginningless, invisible, incomprehensible, unsearchable, immutable, merciful Lord, whom no one has seen, you who alone possess immortality, who dwell in unapproachable light, who created the heaven, the earth, the sea, and all creatures that are in them, who grant to all what they ask before they ask it, we pray and beseech you, Master who loves mankind, Father of our Lord God and Savior Jesus Christ who descended from heaven for the sake of us men and for our salvation, who was incarnate of the Holy Spirit (being born) of Mary, the Mother of God, glorious and virginal; you who taught us, your humble, guilty, and unworthy servants, by your venerable passion, to

Δέξαι ἡμᾶς προσπίπτοντάς σοι καὶ βοῶντας '" τὸ" ἡμάρτομεν Ἐπὶ σὲ ἐπεῤῥίφημεν ἐκ μήτρας ἀπὸ γαστρὸς μητρὸς ἡμῶν' Θεὸς" ἡμῶν εἶ σύ. ᾿Αλλ᾽ ὅτι ἐξέλιπον ἐν ματαιότητι αἱ ἡμέραι ἡμῶν "ἡ, γεγυμνώμεθα τῆς σῆς βοηθείας, ἐστερήμεθα πάσης" ἀπολογίας. ᾿Αλλὰ“ θαῤῥοῦντες τοῖς οἰχτιρμοῖς σου χράζομεν' ἁμαρτίας νεότητος ἡμῶν καὶ ἀγνοίας μὴ μνησθῇς", ἐκ τῶν κρυφίων ἡμῶν καθάρισον ἡμᾶς "5, μὴ ἀποῤῥίψῃς ἡμᾶς εἰς καιρὸν γήρως ἐν τῷ ἐκλείπειν τὴν ἰσχὺν ἡμῶν", μὴ ἐγκαταλίπῃς ἡμᾶς ὁ πρὶν ἡμᾶς εἰς τὴν γῆν ἀποστρέψαι, ἀξίωσον ἡμᾶς πρὸς σὲ ἐπιστρέψαι, καὶ πρόσχες ἡμῖν ἐν εὐμενείᾳ καὶ χάριτι" ἐπιμέτρησον" ταῖς ἀνομίαις ἡμῶν τοὺς οἰκτιρμούς σου ᾽ ἀντίθες τὴν ἄδυσσον τῶν οἰχτιρμῶν σου τῷ πλήθει τῶν πλημμελημάτων ἡμῶν", ἐπίδλεψον ἐξ ὕψους ἁγίου σου ἡ, Κύριε, ἐπὶ τὸν λαόν σου τὸν περιεστῶτα καὶ ἀπεχδεχόμενον τὸ παρὰ σοῦ πλούσιον ἔλεος, ἐπίσχεψαι ἡμᾶς ἐν τῇ χρηστότητί σου, ῥῦσαι ἡμᾶς ἐκ τῆς καταδυναστείας τοῦ διαδόλου, ἀσφάλισαι τὴν ζωὴν ἡμῶν τοῖς ἁγίοις καὶ ἱεροῖς νόμοις σου", ἀγγέλῳ" πιστῷ φύλακι παρακατάθου τὸν λαόν σου, πάντας συνάγαγε εἰς τὴν βασιλείαν σου, δὸς συγγνώμην τοῖς ἐλπίζουσιν ἐπὶ σέ", ἄφες αὐτοῖς καὶ ἡμῖν" ὁ τὰ ἁμαρτήματα, κάθαρον "ἡ ἡμᾶς τῇ ἐνεργείᾳ τοῦ ἁγίου σου Πνεύματος, διάλυσον τὰς ὁ μηχανὰς τοῦ ἐχθροῦ ἢ. Νῦν οὖν δεομένων ἐπάκουσον ἡμῶν ΄, καὶ μνήσθητι ἡμῶν τῶν ταπεινῶν καὶ καταχρίτων, καὶ '" ἐπίστρεψον τὴν αἰμμαλωσίαν τῶν ψυχῶν ἡμῶν, τὴν οἰκείαν συμπάθειαν ἔχων ὑπὲρ ἡμῶν πρεσδεύουσαν. Δέξαι ἡμᾶς προσπίπτοντάς σοι χαὶ βοῶντας '" τὸ" ἡμάρτομεν ᾿Επὶ σὲ ὁ ἐπεῤῥίφημεν ἐκ μήτρας ἀπὸ γαστρὸς μητρὸὴς ἡμῶν" Θεὸς" ἡμῶν εἶ σύ. ᾿Αλλ᾽ ὅτι ἐξέλιπον ἐν ματαιότητι αἱ ἡμέραι ἡμῶν "ἡ, γεγυμνώμεθα τῆς σῆς βοηθείας, ἐστερήμεθα πάσης "" ἀπολογίας. ᾿Αλλὰ“ θαῤῥοῦντες τοῖς οἰκτιρμοῖς σου χράζομεν' ἁμαρτίας γεότητος ἡμῶν καὶ ἀγνοίας μὴ μνησθῇς", ἐκ τῶν χρυφίων ἡμῶν καθάρισον ἡμᾶς δ, μὴ ἀποῤῥίψῃς ἡμᾶς εἰς καιρὸν γήρως ἐν τῷ ἐκλείπειν τὴν ἰσχὺν ἡμῶν“, μὴ ἐγκαταλίπῃς ἡμᾶς ὁ πρὶν ἡμᾶς εἰς τὴν γῆν ἀποστρέψαι, ἀξίωσον ἡμᾶς πρὸς σὲ ἐπιστρέψαι, καὶ πρόσχες ἡμῖν ἐν εὐμενείᾳ καὶ χάριτι" ἐπιμέτρησον ὁ ταῖς ἀνομίαις ἡμῶν τοὺς οἰχτιρμούς σου ᾽ ἀντίθες τὴν ἄδυσσον τῶν οἰχτιρμῶν σου τῷ πλήθει τῶν πλημμελημάτων ἡμῶν", ἐπίδλεψον ἐξ ὕψους ἁγίου σου, Κύριε, ἐπὶ τὸν λαόν σου τὸν περιεστῶτα καὶ ἀπεχδεχόμενον τὸ παρὰ σοῦ πλούσιον ἔλεος, ἐπίσκεψαι ἡμᾶς ἐν τῇ χρηστότητί σου, ῥῦσαι ἡμᾶς ἐκ τῆς καταδυναστείας τοῦ διαθόλου, ἀσφάλισαι τὴν ζωὴν ἡμῶν τοῖς ἁγίοις καὶ ἱεροῖς νόμοις σου δ, ἀγγέλῳ" πιστῷ φύλαχι παρακατάθου τὸν λαόν σου, πάντας συνάγαγε εἰς τὴν βασιλείαν σου, δὸς συγγνώμην τοῖς ἐλπίζουσιν ἐπὶ σέ ἡ, ἄφες αὐτοῖς καὶ ἡμῖν" τὰ ἁμαρτήματα, κάθαρον ἡμᾶς τῇ ἐνεργείᾳ τοῦ ἁγίου σου Πνεύματος, διάλυσον τὰς ὁ μηχανὰς τοῦ ἐχθροῦ ἢ. in a place of coolness, from where pain, affliction, and lamentation have fled, place their spirits in the dwellings of the just, find them worthy of peace and remission, for it is not the dead who will praise you, O Lord, and it is not those who are in hell who will hope in your mercy, but it is we, the living, who will bless you and remember you for our souls. Source that never ceases to flow and well up, life-giving and luminous, creative power coeternal with the Father, who has admirably realized the organization of all things for the salvation of men, Christ our God, who has broken the indissoluble bonds of death and the bolts of hell, who has trampled underfoot the multitude of spirits, who offered yourself as a perfect <offering> for all men, who <have> delivered for us your most pure body, intact, inaccessible to any sin, <by> delivering yourself … <gave us eternal life>, who descended into hell, broke the eternal bolts, and showed those who dwelt below the path to the heights, who by (your) divine skill caught (on the hook) the old evildoer, the dragon of the

Βλελλχιρ κὸ ἐρελραπητελν, πο» πὲ ΘΞΤΆΣΉΒ ἨΛΠΙΗΧΆ, ΓΓΦΟΟΠΟΑΣΒ ΜΗΛΟΟΤῊ, ἠθΑΟΥ ΕΟΈΚΟΜΟΥ ΟΧΜΟΧΥΧΗΟΥ Ἡ ἨΘΟΖΙΚΟΧΥΧΉΟΥ ἵ ΚΟ ΈΚΟΜΟΥ ΟΔΙΒΕΟΤΟΥ ΟΧΑΈΤΕΛΕ, ΟΧΟΤΑΒΛΈΣΜΟΥ ΠΑΆΖΙ ΡΑΞΑΡΟΥΙΠΛΕΜΟΥ ἘΜΒΟΤΟΥ ΘΕΟΥ͂, ἘΗΤΕΙ ΟΥ̓ΧΗΔΑΒΗΟΥΜΟΥ ἵ ΠΡΗΟΤΑΒΛΕΝΕΝ ᾽ν ΛΈΤΟΥ ἹΗΟΛΧΗΟΥΜΟΥ ἜΜΈΧΙΙΝΧ, ἨΗΞΧΒΟΛΑ ΒΚ ΛΑ τ ἘΧΞΒΟΛΑ, ἵ ΟΣ ΒΑΞΑΙΔῊ ΟΛΛΕΟ τ ΦΑΞΑΦΈΙΠΛΙΔΗ ΘΗ ΛΗ, ἨΛΟΤΟΙΑΊΒΑΑ ΠΟΥΡΈΒΧΗΟΜΙ “ἢ ΟΧΜΑΤΡΈΙΑ τ ἘΔΑΆΊΒΑΑ ΟΥ̓ΑΘΕΒ ΟΤφΟμΆ, ΒΒ ΞΕΕΘΕΛΈΙΑ ΟΥ̓ΠΧΒΑΉΜΕΜ, ΟΣΙΡΧΤΕ ΠΟΒΗΉΧΗΣ ΟΑΊΒΕ ΜΗΘΞΈΗΧ ΡΑΗΛΙΧ᾽ τῶι, Βλελλζι» Κὸ, Εὐῖχα Ἐπςο» πε, ὑςχϑπελορε Ἠλπζ, ΟΔΗΜΗΧῚ ΝᾺ Ξ6ΜΠῊ τ ΝᾺ ΜΟΡῊ ἈΛΛΕῚῊΣ, Β΄ ΚΓ ΠΟΟΛΈΛΒΗΜΗ ἵ ΒΕΛΗΒΖΙ τ ὁς ΣΡ ΠΦΑΣΟΧΉΧΗ "ΗΑ πᾶτηκο- ΟΤΉΜΗΧΙ ΘΗΝ ΠΟΚΑΞΑΒΑΙ ἨΛΗΧ ΠΡΟΘΕΡΑΧΕΝῊΣ Ο“ΒΑΣΤΈΗ ἙΑΜΗΘΟΔΙΒΕΗ ἘῊ Ὑ ΟΗῊΗ ΠΡΉΘΝΟ ΟΔΊΒΗΗ ἃ ἨΕΡΑΞΛΑΜΒΗΒΗ τ ἨΣΗΞΙΚἘΜΕΜΈΗ δ, τ ΠΡΗΙΙΕΟΥΕΗῈ “ἢ πηποτπορασ Ἱβλλτο Το ᾿φογσχλ ΒΖ ΘΕΡΑΞΧ ΟΓΉΒΗΟΙΜΣ ἸΑΞΧΊΚΟΝΧ ἨᾺ ΟΞΒΑΣΎΖΙΆ ΤΈΟΙΑ
in a place of coolness, from where pain, affliction, and lamentation have fled, place their spirits in the dwellings of the just, find them worthy of peace and remission, for it is not the dead who will praise you, O Lord, and it is not those who are in hell who will hope in your mercy, but it is we, the living, who will bless you and remember you for our souls.


[p. 797] {#po.24.p797}
ροδυνάμου σου " χατασφαλισάμενος ἰσχύος" ἡ μεγαλώνυμος σοφία τοῦ Πατρός, ὁ τοῖς ἐπηρεαζομένοις μέγας ἐπήκοος ἡ φανείς, καὶ φωτίσας τοὺς ἐν σχότει χαὶ σκιᾷ θανάτου χαθημένους'", σὺ δόξης ἀεννάου Κύριε καὶ Πατρὸς ὑψίστου ΥἹὲ ἀγαπητέ'", ἀίδιον φῶς ἐξ ἀϊδίου φωτός, ἥλιε δικαιοσύνης, ἐπάκουσον ἡμῶν δεομένων σου καὶ ἀνάπαυσον τὰς ψυχὰς τῶν δούλων σου τῶν προκεχοιμημένων, πατέρων ἡμῶν τε'" χαὶ ἀδελφῶν χαὶ λοιπῶν συγγενῶν χατὰ σάρχα καὶ πάντων τῶν οἰκείων τῆς πίστεως περὶ ὧν καὶ τὴν μνήμην ποιούμεθα νῦν" ὅτι ἐν σοὶ πάντων! τὸ κράτος καὶ ἐν τῇ χειρί σου κατέχεις τὰ πέρατα '". Δέσποτα παντοχράτορ, Θεὲ πατέρων καὶ Κύριε τοῦ ἐλέους, γένους θνητοῦ τε καὶ ἀθανάτου καὶ πάσης φύσεως ἀνθρωπίνης ὁ δημιουργέ, συνισταμένης τε χαὶ πάλιν λυομένης ζωῆς τε καὶ τελευτῆς ᾽", τῆς ἐνταῦθα διαγωγῆς, τῆς ἐκεῖθεν μεταστάσεως "ἢ, ὁ χρόνους μετρῶν τοῖς ζῶσι χαὶ χαιροὺς ἱστῶν θανάτου "ἡ, κατάγων εἰς ἅδου καὶ ἀνάγων, δεσμεύων ἐν ἀσθενείᾳ καὶ ἀπολύων ἐν δυναστείᾳ ᾽", ὁ τὰ παρόντα χρησίμως οἰκονομῶν καὶ τὰ μέλλοντα λυσιτελῶς διοικῶν, ὁ τοὺς θανάτου χέντρῳ πληγέντας ἀναστάσεως εὐφραίνων ἐλπίσιν ᾽" αὐτός, Δέσποτα, τῶν ἀπάντων ὁ Θεός, ὁ Σωτὴρ ἡμῶν, ἡ ἐλπὶς πάντων τῶν περάτων τῆς γῆς" καὶ τῶν ἐν θαλάσσῃ μακράν, ὁ καὶ ἐν ταύτῃ τῇ ἐσχάτῃ καὶ μεγάλῃ καὶ σωτηρίῳ ἡμέρᾳ τῆς ἑορτῆς τὸ μυστήριον ᾽" τῆς ἁγίας καὶ ὁμοουσίου καὶ συναϊδίου καὶ ἀδιαιρέτου καὶ ἀσυγχύτου Τριάδος ὑποδείξας ἡμῖν, καὶ τὴν ἐπιφοίτησιν"" χαὶ παρουσίαν τοῦ ἁγίου ὁ χαὶ ζωοποιοῦ σον Πνεύματος ἐν εἴδει πυρίνων γλωσσῶν ἐπὶ τοὺς ἁγίους σου ἀποστόλους ἐχχέας"", καὶ ἀγγελιστὰς αὐτοὺς θέμενος τῆς εὐσεθοῦς πίστεως, χαὶ ὁμολογητὰς χαὶ χήρυχας τῆς ἈΠΦΘΟΤΟΡΑΖΙ ἸΞΛΗΈΛΧ ΕΗ προποκ ΛΑ τα ΟΥ̓ ΠΟΥΒΕΝΗΣ ΑὐΧΠΑΙΒΜΗΝΧ αἰ Αρζπη- ΜΧΙΗΜΧ ἘΦῸΣ ΗΘΜ ΤΕΘΗΜΕ ΞΑΚΟΝΟΛΛΉΜΕΙΕ" ΤΙ ΟὙΕΟ. Ποπεχ» ΤΑῊῪ ΜΟΛΗΤ οΑ᾽ ΒλκλΆζιΣ κὸ Γ ΘΟΠΟΑΣῚ ἘποΣ τὲ ΛΠΙΣ, ΤᾺΘΡΕῪΣ ἘΕΜΟΥ, ΤΕΟΈ ΕΟΤᾺ ΚΔ ΒΟΤΗΗΣ ΒέΔΗΚΑΛ ΤΑΒΗΛ, ἨΧΙΒΈΙΒΒΗΕΕ ΡΑ ΞΑΡΟΥΙΠΕΗΗΕ ὙΒΟΗΧΧ ΤΕΆΡΕΗ α ΤΡΑΛΑΙΒΕΗ ΒΕΥΒΗΣΙ ἢ ποκοη. ἴ... οὗ ἈΧΟΧΟΖΑΘΗΒΗ ἡ ἨΛΙΠΕΜ, ἘΧΙΒΑΙΙΜΗΜΙ Κρ ἢ ΜΗΡῈ τ ὁ ΒΖΟσ ΧοπλημΗ" ΜᾺ ΟΥΠΧΒΑΉΜΕ ἌΗΒΟΤΑ ἘΓΟΚΟΝΕΎΒΝΑΛΤΟ τ ἘΑλαΙμεὲ πο ΒΖΤΟρΟΕ
Master, Lord our God, creator of all things, it is in truth your great mystery, the current decomposition of your creatures and <their future recomposition and their> rest. And <we confess your grace …> for having made us <enter> into <this> world and for making us <exit> from it in the hope of a life without end <at the moment of> the future second coming of
Ὁ Θεὸς ὁ μέγας καὶ αἰώνιος, ὁ ἅγιος καὶ φιλάνθρωπος... Σὸν γὰρ ὡς ἀληθῶς καὶ μέγα ὄντως μυστήριον, Δέσποτα τῶν ἁπάντων χαὶ" ποιητά, ἥ τε πρόσχαιρος λύσις τῶν σῶν χτισμάτων καὶ ἡ μετὰ ταῦτα συνάφεια καὶ ἀνάπαυσις. Καὶ εἰς αἰῶνάς σοι χάριν ἐπὶ πᾶσιν ὁμολογοῦμεν, ἐπὶ ταῖς εἰσόδοις ἡμῶν ταῖς εἰς τὸν κόσμον τοῦτον, καὶ ταῖς ἐξόδοις αἱ" τὰς
your Christ, we magnify even more the communion that you have given us to have with them (= the dead) and that you have realized by descending toward our sufferings and by raising us with you toward your impassibility. Receive therefore, Master, our prayers, and grant rest to all our fathers and to the mothers of each of us, to our brothers, sisters, children, relatives, [friends and] compatriots

ΟΥ̓ΜΗ ἹΜΕΝᾺ ΗΧΧ ἘΖ ἈΧΗΜΤΑΧΧ ἈΗΒΟΥΧΗΧΙΧΆ, ἨᾺ ΔΟΗῈ ΛΕΡΆΜΛΗ, ἰρΑκοκῈ, ΠΈκοπλη, πὰ πὰς ΟΤἘΠ ἘΜΕΔΙΒΜΗΧ, ΒΒ ἨΑΟΛΈΛΒΗ ἩΦἘΡΑΣ ΞΕΡΟΤΕΑ ἩΦΕΡΕΕΡΟΦΕΣΗΛΑΤΟ, τ ἘΚ ΑΗ ΠΗ͂ΜΑ ΒΧΒΟΛΑ Ά. ΟΣ ὉΒΈΤΧΑΧΙΜΗ ΑΠΦΟΟΤΟΣΛΑΙ ἢ ΤΕΘΗΜΗ, ΒΧ Ο“ΚΑΣΤΑΙΧᾺ ὙΒΟΗΧΖ ΟΚΗΤΈΛΕΧΣ. ΟΧΑΑΞΑΒΗΓΗῊ ΤΈΛΕΑ 'δ ἨΛΙΙΑ ἘΜ ΓΗΒ ΒΒ ΒΒπΙΕ ὁςπάΣ το
and to all the souls already asleep in the hope of eternal life. Place their names in the book of life, in the bosom of Abraham, of Isaac, of Jacob, in the place of the living, in the inheritance of the kingdom of heaven, in the paradise of delights, by having them led there by your <angels> of light, into your holy dwellings. Make our bodies rise with (theirs) on the day that you have announced holily and without lie; for there is no death, O Lord, for your servants, who depart from the body and come to you, God of spirits and of all flesh, but a passage from suffering to joy. Those who have sinned against you, be merciful to them, and to us, sinners, because no one will be without stain before you, except you alone who have appeared on earth, Lord Jesus Christ, through whom we all hope to find mercy and the remission of (our) sins. Also, O Lord, to us and to them, forgive,
Ἡ ΜΕΛΟΆΧΗΟ ΠΟΒΈΛΑΛΧ ΕΗ ἨΈΟΥΣ ἘῸ, ΤΦΟΟΠΟΛΣῚ, ΦΑΈΘΗΧ ὙΒΟΉΙΧ ΟΧΜΡΧΤΗ, ἸοχολΑΠ ΒΗ πμΣ ΤᾺ ΤΈΛΕΟΣ τ ΠΡΗΧΟΛΑΙΒΜΜΉΜΧ ΚΔ ΤΕΚῈ, Ἐποσπε Ἀπογσχομ α ἘΦΈΚΟΗ ΠΛΑΤῊ, ἨΖ πρξοτλπλένηε ὡςτχ» "ἡ ΠΕῚΛΛΒΗΧΙΧΑ ΒΖ φΑΛΟΟΤΒΗΛΑ. ἵπιε
absolve and remit, as one who is good and who loves mankind, our faults voluntary and involuntary, conscious and unconscious, known and hidden, committed in action or in word; make free the passages of our souls (?),

and bless us, who are here present, give to us and to all your people a good and peaceful end, and make us worthy of your kingdom at the terrible and frightening tribunal of your Christ. Aloud: For you are the God of mercy and salvation, and we address glory to you, Father, etc.

and to all the souls already asleep in the hope of eternal life. Place their names in the book of life, in the bosom of Abraham, of Isaac, of Jacob, in the place of the living, in the inheritance of the kingdom of heaven, in the paradise of delights, by having them led there by your <angels> of light, in your holy dwellings. Make our bodies rise with (theirs) on the day that you have announced holily and without falsehood; for there is no death, Lord, for your servants, who depart from the body and come to you, God of spirits and of all flesh, but a passage from suffering to joy. Those who have sinned against you, be merciful to them, and to us, sinners, because no one will be without stain before you, except you alone who have appeared on earth, Lord Jesus Christ, through whom we all hope to find mercy and the remission of (our) sins. ἄφες, συγχώρησον τὰ παραπτώματα ἡμῶν τὰ ἑκούσια χαὶ τὰ ἀχούσια, τὰ ἐν γνώσει καὶ ἀγνοίᾳ, τὰ πρόδηλα, τὰ λανθάνοντα, τὰ ἐν πράξει, τὰ ἐν διανοίᾳ "5, τὰ ἐν λόγῳ, τὰ ἐν πάσαις ἡμῶν ταῖς ἀναστροφαῖς καὶ τοῖς κινήμασι" 7" χαὶ τοῖς μὲν προλαθοῦσιν ἐλευθερίαν καὶ ἄνεσιν δώρησαι᾽", ἡμᾶς δὲ τοὺς περιόντας" εὐλόγησον, τέλος ἀγαθὸν καὶ εἰρηνικὸν παρεχόμενος" ἡμῖν τε καὶ παντὶ τῷ λαῷ σου, χαὶ ἐλέους σπλάγχνα καὶ φιλανθρωπίας διανοίγων ἡμῖν" ἐν τῇ φρικτῇ καὶ φοδερᾷ σου παρουσίᾳ "ἡ, καὶ τῆς βασιλείας σον ἀξίους ἡμᾶς ποίησον "ὃ.
